# U2 milestone 1 pension source adjudication

Documentary registry: `data/external/track_u2/pension.json`. 642 RESOLVED entries and 6 TO VERIFY entries. These include 576 individual variable mappings plus the named routes below. No raw PSID values or estimator were read or run.

A resolved variable mapping establishes its label, position, width, codebook domain and printed-page universe. It does not resolve a separate route or relationship blocker. The registry retains full codebook text with published frequency columns removed. `PSID` is `/Users/maxghenis/PolicyEngine/psid-data`. Pages are printed codebook/questionnaire pages; every SPSS line is one-based. The 2013–2019 staging has no separate family SPSS formats file; 2021/2023 formats are checked where a block exists. Numeric amount domains come from codebooks, not absent format blocks.

The 2015 P62ACKPT heading/table ambiguity and male-spouse gate remain explicit refusals. From 2017 the questionnaire explicitly removes the prior formula/DK account branch. The registry documents that change and refuses to treat the inherited generic U2 parameter route as ratified; it needs the specific amendment recorded below.

| `2013.route.current_job` | RESOLVED | P16 identifies formula=1, account=5, both=7, DK=8 and refused=9; P20 is the present account balance for account and combined plans. Use P16, not the later P22 checkpoint, to route P20. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 138; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 140; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2013.route.previous_combined` | RESOLVED | Both previous-employer slots ask P48/P49 only for P46=7 (both). P48=2 is an IRA rollover; P48=3 is left to accumulate. The U2 inherited convention takes only disposition 3. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 146; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2013.route.previous_dc_only` | RESOLVED | P46=5 routes the two previous-employer plan slots through P62ACKPT to P64/P65. P64=3 means left to accumulate and is the counted disposition. P64=2 is an excluded IRA rollover. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2013.route.checkpoint_documentation` | RESOLVED | Exact P62ACKPT wording is retained below; its routing changes across waves and must not be inferred from U1 code constants. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2013.route.formula_unknown_checkpoint` | RESOLVED | Questionnaire P62ACKPT admits P62AMT DK/RF as well as P46=5, whereas P64/P65 codebook Inap. descriptions exclude formula/combined types. U1 explicitly chose questionnaire routing. U2 inherits that decision for its shared 2013 wave. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173`; `docs/design/boomers2004_uniform_cut_comparison.md:599` |
| `2013.route.ira_rollovers` | RESOLVED | P48 and P64 label disposition 2 as rolled over into an IRA and 3 as left to accumulate. Preserve U2 exclusion of disposition 2; this is a registered ownership convention, not a finding from observed account values. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2013.route.duplicate_and_off_route` | RESOLVED | Use combined-plan P49 at most once; never add combined-plan P65 alongside it. Amounts outside the adjudicated current/previous route remain explicit off-route dispositions. The inherited duplicate guard is preserved even where the later instrument no longer re-asks combined accounts. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 145; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 151; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2013.route.amounts_brackets_top_codes` | RESOLVED | P20, P49 and P65 ask balances now and offer follow-up brackets for nonresponse. Exact codebook domains are pinned per variable: current balances NUM(9.0); previous balances NUM(8.0). U2 keeps top codes as recorded, counts DK/refusal as zero with dispositions, and does not use brackets. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 140; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1185`; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 596; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 613; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 641; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 650; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 679; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 680; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 697; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 714; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 741; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 742; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 750; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 751; `PSID/family/2013/FAM2013ER_codebook.pdf` p. 780 |
| `2013.route.previous_plan_universe` | RESOLVED | P45 asks about previous-employer pensions aside from IRA/Keogh plans, expected future benefits, and (under the displayed retirement-income condition) regular payments not yet begun. P69 repeats the plan loop at most twice; no third account balance is collected by that loop. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 144; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 153; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2013.route.respondent_slots` | RESOLVED | The pension loop covers the family-file head/reference-person slot and designated spouse/partner slot. Other family members are not separate pension-loop respondents. P70CKPT supplies the explicit spouse-loop family-list relationship gate. Individual-record role assignment remains the separate U2 role registry task. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 144; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 153; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2013.route.other_current_plan` | RESOLVED | P42 asks about additional tax-deferred plans on the current job; the subsequent P43/P44 questions ask employer contributions and then lead to P45. The U1/U2 balance scope does not invent a second current-job account balance. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 143; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2013.pdf` p. 144; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |

| `2017.route.current_job` | RESOLVED | P16 identifies formula=1, account=5, both=7, DK=8 and refused=9; P20 is the present account balance for account and combined plans. Use P16, not the later P22 checkpoint, to route P20. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 140; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 141; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2017.route.previous_combined` | RESOLVED | Both previous-employer slots ask P48/P49 only for P46=7 (both). P48=2 is an IRA rollover; P48=3 is left to accumulate. The U2 inherited convention takes only disposition 3. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 147; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2017.route.previous_dc_only` | RESOLVED | P46=5 routes the two previous-employer plan slots through P62ACKPT to P64/P65. P64=3 means left to accumulate and is the counted disposition. P64=2 is an excluded IRA rollover. Application refuses pending pension:2017.route.inherited_route_amendment. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 153; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2017.route.checkpoint_documentation` | RESOLVED | Exact P62ACKPT wording is retained below; its routing changes across waves and must not be inferred from U1 code constants. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2017.route.formula_unknown_checkpoint` | RESOLVED | P62ACKPT sends formula P46=1 and all others (combined, DK, refused/NA) to P69; only P46=5 reaches P63–P65. This is an explicit documentary change from U1. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173` |
| `2017.route.inherited_route_amendment` | TO VERIFY | Before registration, replace the generic inherited account/formula/dk route in U2 section 15 with a wave-specific 2017 P46=5-only P64/P65 route, and explicitly disposition all formula/DK/combined amounts as off-route. Independent review must approve that documentary correction; do not silently widen the questionnaire universe. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173` |
| `2017.route.ira_rollovers` | RESOLVED | P48 and P64 label disposition 2 as rolled over into an IRA and 3 as left to accumulate. Preserve U2 exclusion of disposition 2; this is a registered ownership convention, not a finding from observed account values. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 146; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2017.pdf` p. 152; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2017.route.duplicate_and_off_route` | RESOLVED | Use combined-plan P49 at most once; never add combined-plan P65 alongside it. Amounts outside the adjudicated current/previous route remain explicit off-route dispositions. The inherited duplicate guard is preserved even where the later instrument no longer re-asks combined accounts. | `2019.route.current_job` | RESOLVED | P16 identifies formula=1, account=5, both=7, DK=8 and refused=9; P20 is the present account balance for account and combined plans. Use P16, not the later P22 checkpoint, to route P20. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 152; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 153; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2019.route.previous_combined` | RESOLVED | Both previous-employer slots ask P48/P49 only for P46=7 (both). P48=2 is an IRA rollover; P48=3 is left to accumulate. The U2 inherited convention takes only disposition 3. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 157; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 157; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 158; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2019.route.previous_dc_only` | RESOLVED | P46=5 routes the two previous-employer plan slots through P62ACKPT to P64/P65. P64=3 means left to accumulate and is the counted disposition. P64=2 is an excluded IRA rollover. Application refuses pending pension:2019.route.inherited_route_amendment. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 157; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 164; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2019.route.checkpoint_documentation` | RESOLVED | Exact P62ACKPT wording is retained below; its routing changes across waves and must not be inferred from U1 code constants. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2019.route.formula_unknown_checkpoint` | RESOLVED | P62ACKPT sends formula P46=1 and all others (combined, DK, refused/NA) to P69; only P46=5 reaches P63–P65. This is an explicit documentary change from U1. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173` |
| `2019.route.inherited_route_amendment` | TO VERIFY | Before registration, replace the generic inherited account/formula/dk route in U2 section 15 with a wave-specific 2019 P46=5-only P64/P65 route, and explicitly disposition all formula/DK/combined amounts as off-route. Independent review must approve that documentary correction; do not silently widen the questionnaire universe. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173` |
| `2019.route.ira_rollovers` | RESOLVED | P48 and P64 label disposition 2 as rolled over into an IRA and 3 as left to accumulate. Preserve U2 exclusion of disposition 2; this is a registered ownership convention, not a finding from observed account values. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 157; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2019.route.duplicate_and_off_route` | RESOLVED | Use combined-plan P49 at most once; never add combined-plan P65 alongside it. Amounts outside the adjudicated current/previous route remain explicit off-route dispositions. The inherited duplicate guard is preserved even where the later instrument no longer re-asks combined accounts. Application refuses pending pension:2019.route.inherited_route_amendment. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 157; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 157; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 163; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2019.route.amounts_brackets_top_codes` | RESOLVED | P20, P49 and P65 ask balances now and offer follow-up brackets for nonresponse. Exact codebook domains are pinned per variable: current balances NUM(9.0); previous balances NUM(8.0). U2 keeps top codes as recorded, counts DK/refusal as zero with dispositions, and does not use brackets. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 153; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 158; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 164; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1185`; `PSID/family/2019/fam2019er_codebook.pdf` p. 677; `PSID/family/2019/fam2019er_codebook.pdf` p. 692; `PSID/family/2019/fam2019er_codebook.pdf` p. 718; `PSID/family/2019/fam2019er_codebook.pdf` p. 727; `PSID/family/2019/fam2019er_codebook.pdf` p. 755; `PSID/family/2019/fam2019er_codebook.pdf` p. 770; `PSID/family/2019/fam2019er_codebook.pdf` p. 786; `PSID/family/2019/fam2019er_codebook.pdf` p. 812; `PSID/family/2019/fam2019er_codebook.pdf` p. 821; `PSID/family/2019/fam2019er_codebook.pdf` p. 849 |
| `2019.route.previous_plan_universe` | RESOLVED | P45 asks about previous-employer pensions aside from IRA/Keogh plans, expected future benefits, and (under the displayed retirement-income condition) regular payments not yet begun. P69 repeats the plan loop at most twice; no third account balance is collected by that loop. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 156; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 165; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2019.route.respondent_slots` | RESOLVED | The pension loop covers the family-file head/reference-person slot and designated spouse/partner slot. Other family members are not separate pension-loop respondents. P70CKPT supplies the explicit spouse-loop family-list relationship gate. Individual-record role assignment remains the separate U2 role registry task. Application refuses pending roles:2019.relationship.90, roles:2019.relationship.92. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 156; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 165; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2019.route.other_current_plan` | RESOLVED | P42 asks about additional tax-deferred plans on the current job; the subsequent P43/P44 questions ask employer contributions and then lead to P45. The U1/U2 balance scope does not invent a second current-job account balance. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 155; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2019.pdf` p. 156; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |

| `2021.route.current_job` | RESOLVED | P16 identifies formula=1, account=5, both=7, DK=8 and refused=9; P20 is the present account balance for account and combined plans. Use P16, not the later P22 checkpoint, to route P20. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 285; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 287; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2021.route.previous_combined` | RESOLVED | Both previous-employer slots ask P48/P49 only for P46=7 (both). P48=2 is an IRA rollover; P48=3 is left to accumulate. | `2021.route.previous_dc_only` | RESOLVED | P46=5 routes the two previous-employer plan slots through P62ACKPT to P64/P65. P64=3 means left to accumulate and is the counted disposition. Application refuses pending pension:2021.route.inherited_route_amendment. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 294; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 304; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 305; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 305; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |

| `2021.route.formula_unknown_checkpoint` | RESOLVED | P62ACKPT sends formula P46=1 and all others (combined, DK, refused/NA) to P69; only P46=5 reaches P63–P65. This is an explicit documentary change from U1. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 304; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173` |
| `2021.route.inherited_route_amendment` | TO VERIFY | Before registration, replace the generic inherited account/formula/dk route in U2 section 15 with a wave-specific 2021 P46=5-only P64/P65 route, and explicitly disposition all formula/DK/combined amounts as off-route. Independent review must approve that documentary correction; do not silently widen the questionnaire universe. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 304; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1173` |
| `2021.route.ira_rollovers` | RESOLVED | P48 and P64 label disposition 2 as rolled over into an IRA and 3 as left to accumulate. Preserve U2 exclusion of disposition 2; this is a registered ownership convention, not a finding from observed account values. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 295; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 305; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2021.route.duplicate_and_off_route` | RESOLVED | Use combined-plan P49 at most once; never add combined-plan P65 alongside it. Amounts outside the adjudicated current/previous route remain explicit off-route dispositions. The inherited duplicate guard is preserved even where the later instrument no longer re-asks combined accounts. Application refuses pending pension:2021.route.inherited_route_amendment. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 294; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 295; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 304; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 305; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |
| `2021.route.amounts_brackets_top_codes` | RESOLVED | P20, P49 and P65 ask balances now and offer follow-up brackets for nonresponse. Exact codebook domains are pinned per variable: current balances NUM(9.0); previous balances NUM(8.0). U2 keeps top codes as recorded, counts DK/refusal as zero with dispositions, and does not use brackets. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 287; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 295; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 305; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309`; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:1185`; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 724; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 738; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 739; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 765; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 774; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 802; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 817; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 832; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 833; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 859; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 868; `PSID/family/2021/FAM2021ER_codebook.pdf` p. 896 |
| `2021.route.previous_plan_universe` | RESOLVED | P45 asks about previous-employer pensions aside from IRA/Keogh plans, expected future benefits, and (under the displayed retirement-income condition) regular payments not yet begun. Application refuses pending roles:2021.relationship.90, roles:2021.relationship.92. | `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 293; `PSID/documentation/capture1/q2021.pdf` p. 307; `docs/design/boomers2004_1946_55_comparison.md:309` |

Source files and hashes are recorded in the registry `sources` array. The six setup/codebook/questionnaire triplets and two format files were all opened. U1 source was parsed as text/AST only to check the independently extracted 2013 IDs and labels. No U1 registry or wave constant was edited.

Exposure note: one exploratory 2023 codebook extract, printed pages 875–877, incidentally displayed published whole-file frequency columns. They were not used or reproduced in this adjudication. Subsequent automated codebook extraction removed those columns before review. The U1 specification itself also contains historical structural counts; those are inherited documentary exposure, not new calculations or routing evidence.
